  Have: forall i_2 : Z. let x_2 = Mint_0[shift_sint32(a, i_2)] in
      ((0 <= i_2) -> ((i_2 < a_len_0) -> ((x_2 = 0) \/ (x_2 = (1 + i_2))))).
  (* Invariant *)

  (* Invariant *)
  Have: 0 <= a_len_0.
  (* Invariant *)
  Have: 0 <= k.
  (* Invariant *)
  Have: 0 <= i.
  (* Invariant *)
  Have: 0 < a_len_0.
  (* Invariant *)
  Have: forall i_2 : Z. ((i_2 < a_len_0) -> ((i <= i_2) ->
      (a_1[shift_sint32(b, i_2)] = a_1[shift_sint32(a, i_2)]))).
  (* Invariant *)
  Have: forall i_2 : Z. let a_3 = a_1[shift_sint32(a, i_2)] in
      ((i_2 < a_len_0) -> ((i <= i_2) -> ((a_3 = 0) \/ (a_3 = (1 + i_2))))).
  (* Invariant *)
  Have: forall i_2 : Z. ((0 <= i_2) -> ((i_2 < i) ->
      (a_1[shift_sint32(b, i_2)] = (1 + i_2)))).
  (* Invariant *)
  Have: forall i_2 : Z. ((0 <= i_2) -> ((i_2 < i) ->
      (a_1[shift_sint32(b, i_2)] = a_1[shift_sint32(a, i_2)]))).
  (* Invariant *)
  Have: forall i_2 : Z. ((0 <= i_2) -> ((i_2 < i) ->
      (0 < a_1[shift_sint32(a, i_2)]))).
  (* Invariant *)
